於香港註冊成立的有限公司 Incorporated in Hong Kong with limited liability   | 股份代號 Stock Code : 00291
                     決戰高端 聚勢而強
         CONVERGENCE TO STRENGTHEN AND
        ACHIEVE SUCCESS IN PREMIUMIZATION


          本次年報主題全方位闡述了本集團於二零二一年的戰略執行情況：
 配合第二個三年「決戰高端」的策略，本集團依託自身優勢，積極擁抱啤酒行業升級和
品類多元化的新時代，從而「聚勢」；並持續完善中國與國際雙品牌的啤酒組合，成功建立
  高端大客戶平台，進一步提升成本效益等，從而「強大」。本集團已做好充分準備，
     「聚勢而強」，在第三個三年裡「決勝高端」，成為啤酒新世界中的領導者！

        The title of the annual report describes how the Group executed its
                         strategy in 2021 in all dimensions:
    In order to align with the strategy of “achieving success in premiumization”
for the second three-year period, the Group relied on its unique advantages, actively
    embraced the new era of beer industry upgrade and category diversification,
        so as to converge various industry trends and own advantages which
empowers the Group for further development. Meanwhile, the Group also strived to
         strengthen itself by establishing a more robust brand portfolio with
    both Chinese and international brands, successfully creating a premium key
 customers platform and further enhancing its cost-effectiveness. The Group is well-
 prepared to “convergence to strengthen” and “win at premium segment” during
the third three-year period to become the leader of the new world of beer industry.

                        大事記
    2021                MAJOR EVENTS


    里程碑
    MILESTONES


    於1月22日，本公司透過全資附屬公司與華
    潤置地有限公司（股份代號：01109.HK）全資
    附屬公司訂立投資合作協議、搬遷補償協議
    和代建服務合同，發展華潤雪花啤酒（中國）
    有限公司（「華潤雪花」）位於深圳市寶安區的
    地塊。相關協議於3月29日的股東特別大會
    獲股東大比數通過。華潤雪花總部基地暨華
    潤雪花小鎮建設項目已於8月正式啟動。

    On 22 January, the Company, through its wholly-owned subsidiaries, entered into the Joint Venture Agreement,
    the Relocation Compensation Agreement and the Construction Agreement with the wholly-owned subsidiaries of
    China Resources Land Limited (HKEX stock code: 01109.HK) to develop the land owned by China Resources Snow
    Breweries (China) Co., Ltd. (華潤雪花啤酒（中國）有限公司) (“CR Snow”) in Baoan District, Shenzhen. The relevant
    agreements were approved by a majority of shareholders at an extraordinary general meeting held on 29 March.
    The construction of the CR Snow headquarters and CR Snow beer town was officially launched in August.




                                                                       於11月19日，恒生指數有限公司宣佈，本公司獲
                                                                       納入恒生指數成份股，並於12月6日起正式生效。
                                                                       是次本公司再次躋身成為藍籌股，反映資本市場對
                                                                       本集團業務的高度肯定，以及對本集團未來發展前
                                                                       景充滿信心。

                                                                       On 19 November, Hang Seng Indexes Company
                                                                       Limited announced that the Company has been
                                                                       selected as one of the constituent stocks in the
                                                                       Hang Seng Index with effect from 6 December.
                                                                       The re-introduction of the Company as a blue-chip
                                                                       stock reflects the high recognition of the Group’s
    於8月26日，本公司附屬公司華潤酒業控股有限公                                            business and the confidence in the Group’s future
    司（「酒業控股」）與山東景芝白酒有限公司（「山東景                                          development prospects by the capital market.
    芝白酒」）及山東景芝酒業股份有限公司訂立協議，
    並於10月27日正式通過酒業控股向山東景芝白酒
    注資人民幣13億元以收購其40%的股權。

    On 26 August, China Resources Wine Holdings Co.,
    Ltd (華潤酒業控股有限公司) (“WH”), a subsidiary
    of the Company, entered into an agreement with
    Shandong Jingzhi Baijiu Co., Ltd. (山東景芝白酒有
    限公司) (“Shandong Jingzhi Baijiu”) and Shandong
    Jingzhi Liquor Co., Ltd. (山東景芝酒業股份有限
    公司). The Group formally acquired a 40% equity
    interest in Shandong Jingzhi Baijiu through WH by
    injecting RMB1.3 billion on 27 October.

                                                             花之路」，拉開本集團啤酒生產管理升級序幕，
                                                             向啤酒「新世界」生產領導者邁進。



                                                                                                                     致我們的股東
                                                             The Group pioneered the “Snow Road”, a
                                                             unique characteristic of CRSB, to kick off
                                                             the upgrade of the Group’s beer production
本 集 團 成 立「 華 潤 雪 花 技 術 研 究 院 」， 聚 焦 啤 酒 研 發                  management and move towards the leader of
                                                             the “new world” in beer production.



                                                                                                                      To Our Shareholders
創新和科技成果轉化，聯合啤酒產業鏈上下游生態夥
伴，共同打造世界一流且具有國際影響力的「中國啤
酒」科技創新平台。

The Group established the “CRSB Technology Research
Institute” to focus on beer research, innovation
and transformation of technological achievements,
integrates upstream and downstream enterprises on
the beer industrial chain and jointly develop a world-
class and international influential “Chinese Beer”

新產品、重大市場活動
NEW PRODUCTS AND MAJOR MARKETING ACTIVITIES


本集團於2021年持續推進多元品牌組合建設：2月推出零酒精碳酸飲料「雪花小啤汽」；4月推出超高端新品「醴」
和首款果啤產品「黑獅果啤」（#485玫瑰紅），引進國際品牌「紅爵」啤酒；並於10月引進國際品牌「悠世」。

                                                         The Group continued to build and promote its
                                                         diversified brand portfolio in 2021. In February, the

                                                         alcohol was launched. In April, a super-premium
                                                         new product, “Li”, and the first fruity beer product
                                                         “Löwen Fruit Beer” (#485 Rosé) were launched. The

                                                                            二 零 二 一 年 年 報 | Annual Report 2021   7
    本集團於2021年舉辦多個市場推廣活動：4月「雪花硬核啤酒公園」二期開業，持續塑造西安特色地標；7月「雪
    花皖美生活節」在安徽合肥啟幕，燃爆霸都潮流打卡地；「成都雪花啤酒嘉年華」開幕，打造川內知名夜經濟活動
    品牌，曝光量過億；9月「第二屆雪花深圳國際啤酒節」震撼來襲，創建特色「頑啤嘉年華」。

    The Group launched a number of marketing activities in 2021. In April, the second phrase of “Snow Hard Core Beer
    Park” opened, continuing to build a distinctive landmark in Xi’an. In July, the “Snow Wan Mei Life Festival” was
    launched in Hefei, Anhui, igniting a trendy destination in the city; the “Chengdu Snow Beer Carnival” was opened,
    creating a well-known night-time economic event brand in Sichuan with over 100 million exposures. In September,
    “The Second Snow Shenzhen International Beer Festival” was launched, creating a unique “Naughty Beer Carnival”.




    6 月 ，「 喜 力 ® 同 場 ， 世 界 同 頻 」歐 洲 杯 品 牌 體 驗 活 動 全 國 火 熱
    來 襲 。12 月 ，「 喜 力 ® 星 電 音 」2021 第 一 屆 電 子 音 樂 行 業 峰 會 ，
    攜手電音產業鏈相關方一同成立了中國電子音樂共創聯盟，助力
    中國啤酒高質量化發展。

    In June, the UEFA Euro 2020 brand experience campaign
    “HEINEKEN® X UEFA Champions League” was launched
    nationwide. In December, the first electronic music industry
    summit 2021 “Heineken® Soundscape” was held, joining hands
    with stakeholders in the electronic music industry chain to
    establish the China Electronic Music Co-creation Alliance, helping
    to develop high-quality beer business in China.




                                                      「勇闖天涯superX」、「雪花馬爾斯綠」、「黑獅白啤」相繼官宣新代

「中國酒業科技突出貢獻獎」。12月，「消費升級下的啤酒瓶設
計與質量保障關鍵技術研究與應用」項目被中國酒業協會鑒定
為「國際先進水平」。

     財務表現、股東權益、一般業務狀況及策                                 circumstances. In proposing any dividend payout, the board of

情況，境內新冠肺炎疫情反覆，疫情防控   challenges of prevention and control in foreign countries, as well

件，可能造成員工身心健康與生命安全受   situation of prevention and control of the pandemic is still critical.
到影響，企業正常生產經營秩序受到影響   In the event of a public health emergency, both physical and
的風險。                 mental health and life safety of employees may be threatened, as
                     well as the normal production and operation of the corporate may

確保其成功增長和提升股東價值的重要基                    corporate governance framework is essential to the successful
礎。本公司致力達致和保持高水平的企業                    growth of the Company and the enhancement of shareholder
管治，所採納的企業管治原則，強調優質                    value. The Company is committed to attaining and maintaining
的董事會、向所有利益群體負責、開放溝                    high standards of corporate governance and adopts principles of
通和公平披露。                               corporate governance emphasising a quality Board, accountability
                                      to all stakeholders, open communication and fair disclosure.

     司業務的要求。                                            perspectives appropriate to the requirements of the Company’s

     風險管理及內部監控                                          RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
     董事會全面負責建立及維持穩健的風險管                                 The Board assumes overall responsibility to establish and
     理、內部監控及管治制度，為不會有重大                                 maintain sound systems in risk management, internal control
     的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保                                 and governance to provide reasonable, though not absolute,
     證，並管理而非消除未能達到業務目標的                                 assurance against material misstatement or loss and to manage
     風險。董事會確認，建立及有效地執行風                                 rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
     險管理及內部監控制度，確保業務能夠暢                                 objectives. The Board confirms that it is the overall responsibility
     順運作、保障本集團資產和股東權益、確                                 of the Board to establish and effectively implement the risk
     保財務報表可靠，乃董事會的整體責任。                                 management and internal control systems to ensure the smooth
                                                        operation of business, safeguard the Group’s assets and
                                                        shareholders’ interests as well as ensure the reliability of financial
                                                        statements.

     風險管理及內部監控概覽                                        OVERVIEW OF RISK MANAGEMENT AND
                                                        INTERNAL CONTROL
     本 集 團 採 用 與 美 國 Committee of                       The Group adopts the control framework consistent with the
     Sponsoring Organisations of the                    frameworks recommended by the Committee of Sponsoring
     Treadway Commission及香港會計師公會                        Organisations of the Treadway Commission in the United States
     建議一致的監控架構，作為本集團公司風                                 and the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants
     險管理及監控制度的標準。本集團的風險                                 as the standards in establishing risk management and
     管理及內部監控制度包含五個主要元素，                                 control systems among our Group companies. The Group’s
     即有效的監控環境、風險管理、通訊與信                                 risk management and internal control systems include five
     息系統、具有成本效益的監控活動及監察                                 key elements, namely the existence of an effective control
     機制。本集團風險管理及內部監控的具體                                 environment, risk management, communication and information
     實施工作主要由管理層和員工共同負責。                                 system, cost effective control activities and monitoring
                                                        mechanism. The specific implementation tasks of the Group’s
                                                        risk management and internal control are mainly shared by the
                                                        management and employees.

     管理層主要負責設計、執行和維持內部監                                 The management is primarily responsible for the design,
     控措施。本集團已制訂政策及程序，以向                                 implementation, and maintenance of internal controls. The
     全體員工傳達傳達管理層指令及監控業務                                 Group has established policies and procedures to communicate
     活動。相關監控活動包括批准和核實、審                                 management instructions to all employees, and monitor business
     查、資產保護和職責分離。我們的內部監                                 operations. The monitoring activities include approval and
     控自我評估流程，要求各個業務單位評估                                 verification, review, asset protection, and division of duties.
     內部監控的成效，及時制定緩解措施，減                                 Our internal self-assessment monitoring process requires each
     低已識別的重大風險。                                         business unit to evaluate the effectiveness of internal controls,

      二. 主要會計政策                                          2.    PRINCIPAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES
      甲     綜合入賬                                         A     CONSOLIDATION
            本集團之綜合財務報告包括本公司及                                   The consolidated financial statements of the Group include
            其全部直接及間接附屬公司之財務報                                   the financial statements of the Company and of all its
            告。於年內收購或出售之附屬公司之                                   direct and indirect subsidiaries. The results of subsidiaries

    下，方會於綜合財務報表中確認與聯                  recognised in the consolidated financial statements only to

    溢利及虧損。                            are not related to the Group.

            對於以攤餘成本計量的金融資產計量                                   The Group assesses on a forward looking basis the expected
            和以公允價值計量且其變動計入其他                                   credit losses associated with its debt instruments carried at

            期信用損失做出前瞻性評估。減值方                                   applied depends on whether there has been a significant
            法取決於其信用風險是否顯著增加。                                   increase in credit risk.

            就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認                                   For internal credit risk management, the Group considers
            為，違約事件在內部制訂或得自外界                                   an event of default occurs when information developed
            來源的資料顯示債務人不大可能悉數                                   internally or obtained from external sources indicates that
            向債權人（包括本集團）還款（未計及                                  the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the
            本集團所持任何抵押品）時發生。無                                   Group, in full (without taking into account any collaterals
            論上述情形如何，本集團認為，當工                                   held by the Group). Irrespective of the above, the Group
            具 逾 期 超 過 90 天 ， 則 發 生 違 約 ， 除                     considers that default has occurred when a financial asset is
            非本集團有合理且可支持的資料證明                                   more than 90 days past due unless the Group has reasonable
            更滯後的違約標準屬更合適。                                      and supportable information to demonstrate that a more
                                                               lagging default criterion is more appropriate.

            倘有資料顯示對手方面對嚴重財務困                                   The Group writes off a financial asset when there is
            難，且實際上並無收回款項的前景如                                   information indicating that the counterparty is in severe
            對手方被清盤或進入破產程序，本集                                   financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of
            團一般會進行撇銷。於適當時，在考                                   recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been
            慮法律意見後，本集團可能仍會根據                                   placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy
            收款程序處理被撇銷的金融資產。所                                   proceedings. Financial assets written off may still be subject
            收回的任何款項於損益中確認。                                     to enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery
                                                               procedures, taking into account legal advice where
                                                               appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition event.
                                                               Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.

            僅就貿易應收款項而言，本集團應用                                   For trade receivables, the Group applies the simplified
            簡化方法對香港財務報告準則第9號                                   approach in measuring expected credit losses prescribed by
            規定的預期信貸虧損作出撥備。管理                                   HKFRS 9. Management considers the debtors do not contain

            整個存續期的預期虧損。                                        lifetime expected losses.

    可收回金額的計算方法採用根據管理                    The calculation of recoverable amounts are using cash flow
    層通過之五年期財務預算而作出之預                    forecast projections based on financial budgets approved
    計現金流量，採用預計收入增長率及                    by management, cash flows covering five-year period are
    年折讓率等重大假設引伸計算。主要                    extrapolated using the key assumptions including expected


                                                                                                          致我們的股東
    假設的合理變動將不會導致各現金流                    revenue growth rates and a discount rate. A reasonably
    產生單位的可收回金額跌至低於其賬                    possible change in a key assumption would not cause the
    面值。                                 recoverable amount to fall below the carrying amount of the
                                        respective CGU.

            與 客 戶 在 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 15 號                     Upon entering into a contract with a customer with the

            或提供服務之履約責任。該等權利與                                   obligations to transfer goods or provide services to the
            履約責任的結合會產生淨資產或淨負                                   customer. The combination of those rights and performance
            債，取決於剩餘權利與履約責任的關                                   obligations gives rise to a net asset or a net liability
            係。倘剩餘權利的計量超過餘下履約                                   depending on the relationship between the remaining

            之，倘餘下履約責任之計量超過餘下                                   recognised if the measure of the remaining rights exceeds
            權利之計量，則確認為合約負債。                                    the measure of the remaining performance obligations.
                                                               Conversely, contract liabilities are recognised if the measure
                                                               of the remaining performance obligations exceeds the
                                                               measure of the remaining rights.

    貸風險，審批信貸限額前必先進行信           credit history. To minimise its credit risk exposure, credit

      丙     利率風險                                               C       INTEREST RATE RISK
            本集團承受與已抵押銀行結存及租賃                                           The Group is exposed to fair value interest rate risk in
            負債有關的公允價值利率風險。本集                                           relation to pledged bank deposits and lease liabilities. The
            團亦面對與浮動利率銀行結存有關的                                           Group is also exposed to cash flow interest rate risk in
            現金流量利率風險。為平衡利率風                                            relation to variable-rate bank balances. To cover interest rate
            險，本集團以優先固定╱浮動利率組                                           risk, the Group maintains the loan portfolio in a preferred
            合維持貸款組合，並定期進行檢討。                                           fixed/floating interest rate mix and review regularly.

      五. 資本風險管理                                          5.    CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT
            本集團實行資本管理的目的，是要確                                   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in
            保集團內實體能持續經營，同時通過                                   the Group will be able to continue as a going concern
            債務與權益結餘的優化，提高利益群                                   while maximising the return to stakeholders through the
            體的回報。本集團整體策略與前一年                                   optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s
            度並無不同。                                             overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.

          本集團的資本結構包括現金及現金等                                     The capital structure of the Group consists of debt, which
          價物、本公司權益持有人應佔權益                                      includes cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable
          （包括綜合權益變動表所披露的已發                                     to equity holders of the Company, comprising issued capital,
          行股本、儲備及保留溢利）。                                        reserves and retained profits as disclosed in the consolidated
                                                               statement of changes in equity.

            本集團管理層定期審議資本結構。本                                   The management of the Group reviews the capital structure
            集團考慮資本成本及各類資本的相關                                   regularly. The Group considers the cost of capital and
            風險，並通過支付股息、發行新股、                                   the risks associated with each class of capital, and will
            以至作出銀行借貸或贖回現有債務等                                   balance its overall capital structure through the payment of
            方式，對整體資本結構作出平衡。                                    dividends, new share issues as well as the raise of bank loans
                                                               or the redemption of existing debt.
